x ≥ 0

Constraints A_ub x + b_ub M ≤ 0: rows for each lag k∈A: C_k x - M ≤0 => row [row_k, -1] ≤0.

Equality A_eq = ones (n) = S for x, M no eq.
Bounds: x (0, None), M (0, None).

Maybe we can use "exponentiated gradient" for original nonconvex:
- Initialize a (central binomial or given).
- Iterations:
  - c=a*a, M=max c.
  - weights w_k ∝ exp(β(c_k - M)) (or uniform over top peaks).
  - r_i = ∑ w_k a_{k-i} (conv/corr).
  - update a_i ← a_i / (1 + η r_i) or exp(-η r_i), then normalize sum S.
This is a kind of infimal? For linear objective fixed a, this update reduces max(a*a)? At least subgradient. Choose η adaptively with line search.
This is cheap and can run millions. It may converge to local optimum. We can combine with periodic annealing/restarts.

2. `grad_corr(weights, kernel, fsize=None)`:
`kernel` length n, `weights` length L=2n-1. Return grad length n as ∑_k weights_k kernel_{k-i}. Use convolution of kernel and reversed weights. Need fsize >= n+L-1 =3n-2. We can compute:
```
L = len(kernel)*2 -1
N = next_pow2(len(kernel)+L-1)
wr = weights[::-1]
C = real(ifft(fft(kernel,N)*fft(wr,N)))
return C[len(kernel)-1 : 2*len(kernel)-1][::-1]  # indices n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive length n
```
Check slice: `C[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]` length n because 2n-1 - (n-1)=n. Yes.
Weights sum 1. If weights nonnegative.

Alternative for self gradient: kernel=a, weights w, grad=2*grad_corr(w,a). For fixed linear kernel b, gradient=grad_corr(w,b).

3. `project_simplex(v, S)`:
Use(sort cumsum) as before. Need handle S positive.
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
    n=len(v); v=np.asarray(v,float)
    if S <=0: return zeros? but S positive.
    # If sum <=S and all>=0? Then scaling? Project onto {x>=0,sum=S}.
    # Simple algorithm: sort descending.
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.arange(1,n+1)
    theta=(css - S)/rho
    cond = theta <= u
    idx=np.where(cond)[0]
    if len(idx)==0: rho=n
    else: rho=idx[-1]+1
    theta=(css[rho-1]-S)/rho
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
This standard. For n=16384 sort ok.

6. LSE helpers:
```
def lse_weights(c, beta):
    cm = c.max()
    z = beta*(c-cm)
    # clamp z min maybe -700, max? no need
    e = np.exp(np.maximum(z, -745))
    tot=e.sum()
    if tot <=0: return np.ones(len(c))/len(c), cm
    return e/tot, cm
```

Let's detail LP primal:
```
def lp_best_response(b, A_indices, S, tol=1e-8):
    n=len(b); m=len(A_indices)
    # Build A_ub m x (n+1): rows for lags in A: row[k, i]=? b_{k-i}? For c_k=∑_i b_{k-i} a_i? If b fixed and variable x, c_k =∑_{i=0}^{n-1} b_{k-i} x_i where b index valid. row[i] = b[k-i] if 0<=k-i<n else 0.
    A = np.zeros((m,n+1))
    rhs = np.zeros(m) # <=0 for A x + [-1]M <=0
    for row_idx,k in enumerate(A_indices):
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(k,n-1)
       js=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
       A[row_idx, js] = b[k-js]
       A[row_idx, n] = -1.0
    # equality: sum x = S
    Aeq = np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,:n]=1
    bvec=np.array([S])
    cvec=np.zeros(n+1); cvec[n]=1
    bounds = [(0,None)]*n + [(0,None)]
    res=linprog(cvec,A_ub=A,b_ub=rhs,A_eq=Aeq,b_eq=bvec,bounds=bounds,method='highs')
```
If res.success, x=res.x[:n], M=res.x[-1]. Clean x max0, rescale? It should satisfy sum S. Project to be safe: x=project_simplex(x,S), but that may violate constraints; for use as direction, normalize sum. Maybe if projection changes little, ok. We can use x as g.
Performance: Building dense A each LP O(mn). For m=200,n=8192 ~1.6M operations, ok. Linprog time unknown. To avoid huge A for n=16384 m=200 ~3.2M, ok. Use m ≤300. Could be okay.

But if we call many, may be slow. Use only a few (e.g., 20-50) with time checks.

def lp_improve(a, S, max_time):
    n=len(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_score=2*n*conv(a,a).max()/S² (or max if S²=2n)
    start=time
    A_indices = top_lags(conv(a,a), K=80)
    for call in range(max_calls):
        if timeLeft < min LP: break
        g = solve_lp_best_response(a, A_indices, S, time_limit)
        if g is None: break
        # line search t values
        candidates=[a, g, 0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5]
        for t...
           cand=(1-t)*a + t*g; cand=project_simplex(max(cand,0),S)
           sc = max conv
           if sc<best_score: best...
        # update a to best candidate if improved else keep? If no improvement, maybe keep? To avoid cycling, if no improve after few, break or perturb.
        # update active indices based on c=a*g? top K of c or of self? Use top of max(self, cross).
        c = conv(a,g)
        A_indices = top union of A_indices and top c where c> best? maybe top K=150.
        if a changed significantly continue else break
```

Now self FISTA. We can base on previous but simpler, with line search? Let's implement `optimize_self(a, S, max_time, rng)`:
- a normalized S. best_a,best_score.
- y=a.copy(); t=1.0; beta=BETA_INIT (e.g., 10 or 20); iteration.
- At each iter:
  c=conv(y,y), M=c.max(). update best.
  weights w = lse(c, beta)
  r=grad_corr(w,y) (length n)
  grad=2*r
  # choose step
  l2sq = np.dot(y,y)
  step = 0.8 / (beta * max(1.0, l2sq))
  # If no improvement for many, reduce beta? Actually beta increases to sharpen.
  beta = min(beta_max, beta*(1+0.001))? Previous exponential. Use beta = min(beta_max, beta + beta_rate). Let's use beta = min(beta_max, beta*1.001).
  # FISTA update:
  x = project_simplex(y - step*grad, S)
  t_new=0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4*t*t))
  y = x + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x-a_old); y=max(y,0)
  a_old=x
  t=t_new
  # Periodic multiplicative repulsion to escape:
  if it%500==0:
      w2=lse(c, beta*0.5) maybe
      r2=grad_corr(w2,y)
      rnorm=max(r2.max(),1e-12)
      lr=0.05
      y *= np.exp(-lr*r2/rnorm); y=project_simplex(y,S); t=1.0; a_old=y? (set momentum reset)
  # Periodic noise early/late:
  if it%2000==0:
      scale=0.01*S/np.sqrt(n)*np.exp(-it/(5*max(1,it_decay)))
      if scale>1e-6: y=project_simplex(y+rng.normal(0,scale,n),S); t=1.0
```

Maybe simpler projected gradient with backtracking:

def top_indices(values, k, existing=None):
    n=len(values)
    k=min(k,n)
    if k<=0: return np.array([],dtype=int)
    if existing is None or len(existing)==0:
        if k==n: return np.arange(n)
        idx=np.argpartition(values, -k)[-k:]
        idx=idx[np.argsort(values[idx])[::-1]]
        return idx
    else:
        mask=np.zeros(n,bool)
        mask[existing]=True
        if len(existing)>=k:
            # keep top k among existing? But want include existing plus new? We'll construct candidate set existing + top k of values.
            pass
        cand_set=set(existing.tolist())
        if len(cand_set)<k:
            # add top of values not in set until k
            # get top 2k all
            k2=min(n, max(k-len(cand_set), k))
            idx=np.argpartition(values, -k2)[-k2:]
            for i in idx:
                cand_set.add(int(i))
                if len(cand_set)>=k: break
        else:
            # choose top k overall from existing? But we may want to keep those with high cross. Just top k by values among existing maybe.
            arr=np.array(list(cand_set))
            if len(arr)>k:
                sub=np.argpartition(values[arr], -k)[-k:]
                arr=arr[sub]
            return arr[np.argsort(values[arr])[::-1]]
        # if still <k add top overall
        idx=np.argpartition(values, - (k-len(cand_set)+10))[- (k-len(cand_set)+10):]
        for i in idx: cand_set.add(int(i))
        arr=np.array(list(cand_set))
        return arr
```

def optimize_self(a, S, max_time, rng, beta_init=20.0, beta_max=1000.0, use_noise=True):
    n=len(a); deadline=time.monotonic()+max_time
    a=normalize_sum(a,S)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_m=self_max(a)
    y=a.copy(); v=a.copy(); t=1.0; beta=beta_init; it=0; last_improve=0; plateau=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       it+=1
       c=self_conv(y); M=c.max()
       if M < best_m - 1e-12:
          best_m=M; best_a=y.copy(); last_improve=it
       # If stuck, perturb
       if it - last_improve > 2000:
          # noise scale adaptive
          scale = 0.005*S/np.sqrt(n)*np.exp(-it/10000)
          if scale>1e-7:
             y=project_simplex(y + rng.normal(0,scale,n),S)
             v=y.copy(); t=1.0; last_improve=it; beta=min(beta, beta_init*4)
          else:
             break
       w = lse_weights(c,beta)
       r=grad_corr(w,y)
       # multiplicative escape every 400?
       if it % 400 == 0:
          lr=0.03
          rmax = r.max()
          if rmax > 1e-12:
             y = y * np.exp(-lr*r/rmax)
             y = project_simplex(y,S)
             v=y.copy(); t=1.0
       # FISTA step
       step = 0.5/(beta * max(1.0, np.dot(y,y)))
       x=project_simplex(y - step*2? r? wait gradient for self=2r. We can define grad=2*r. Use step with grad.
       grad=2.0*r
       x=project_simplex(y - step*grad, S)
       t_new=0.5*(1+np.sqrt(1+4*t*t))
       y=x + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x-v)
       np.maximum(y,0,out=y)
       v=x; t=t_new
       beta=min(beta_max, beta*1.002)
    # final check
    M_best=self_conv(best_a).max(); return best_a,best_m
```

`_next_pow2(m)`:
```
def _next_pow2(m):
    m=int(m)
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

def _fft_conv(a,b):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); b=np.asarray(b,float)
    out=a.size+b.size-1
    n=_next_pow2(out)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,n)? For real, use rfft/irfft for speed. Need both fft? Use np.fft.rfft and irfft.
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,n); B=np.fft.rfft(b,n); c=np.fft.irfft(A*B,n)[:out]
    return c
```

def _corr_with_weights(w, kernel):
    n=kernel.size; L=2*n-1
    out=n+L-1
    N=_next_pow2(out)
    wr=w[::-1]
    A=np.fft.rfft(kernel,N); B=np.fft.rfft(wr,N); d=np.fft.irfft(A*B,N)
    return d[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
Check slice length `2*n-1 - (n-1) = n`. Good.

`_project_simplex(v, S)`:
Need handle if S very small; but in optimization S>0. If v contains NaN? return uniform.
```
def _project_simplex(v, S):
    n=len(v); v=np.asarray(v,float)
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    if S<=0: return np.zeros(n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        v=np.zeros(n)
    # Quick if sum exactly S and min>=0: return v
    s=v.sum()
    if s<=0:
        return np.full(n, S/n)
    if np.min(v)>=0 and abs(s-S)<=1e-12:
        return v
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    theta=(css - S)/rho
    cond=theta <= u
    if not np.any(cond):
        r=n
    else:
        r=int(np.where(cond)[0][-1])+1
    theta=(css[r-1]-S)/r
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
```
This is O(n log n). Could use `scipy.optimize`? fine.

`_normalize(a,S,maxval=1000.0)`:
```
def _normalize(a,S):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12:
        return np.full(len(a), S/len(a))
    return a*(S/s)
```
But if after scaling values >1000? If shape has very large relative, scaling to S may still <1000. We can if max >1000 scale down to 1000 (score unchanged? ```
def _lp_improve(a,S,max_time):
    n=len(a); deadline=time.monotonic()+max_time
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_score(a,S)
    c=self_conv(a)
    K=64
    no_improve=0
    call=0
    start_len=len(a)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and call < 200:
        if K < 16: K=16
        if K > 2*n-2: K=2*n-2
        A_idx=top_indices(c, K)
        t0=time.monotonic()
        g=Mlp_best_response(a,S,A_idx)
        dt=time.monotonic()-t0
        call+=1
        if g is None:
            if dt > 5: break
            K=max(32,K//2); continue
        # line search
        best_t=0; best_cand=best_a
        for t in (0.05,0.1,0.15,0.2,0.3,0.4,0.5,0.65,0.8,1.0):
            cand=(1-t)*a + t*g
            cand=np.maximum(cand,0)
            cand=_project_simplex(cand,S)
            sc=_score(cand,S)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score=sc; best_a=cand.copy(); best_t=t; break? We can continue to find better t but break may miss smaller t better. We'll evaluate all and choose best.
        if best_t>0:
            a=best_a.copy()
            c=self_conv(a)
            no_improve=0
        else:
            no_improve+=1
            if no_improve>=4:
                # maybe try larger K once
                K=min(2*n-1,K+32)
                if K>256: break
        # adjust K based on dt
        if dt < 1.0 and K<128: K=min(2*n-1,K+16)
        elif dt > 5.0 and K>64: K=max(32,K//2)
        # c for active from cross? We can update c as self? Active constraints for best response to a should be lags of a*g (cross), not self.
        if best_t>0 or True:
            cross=_fft_conv(a,g)
            c_active=cross # top constraints for next LP
            # But if no improvement, still use cross of current a and g? g best response to old a.
        # keep self c for initial? We can use c_active for next top.
        c_active_full=cross
        c=c_active_full
    return best_a,best_score
```
Need be careful: LP's active constraints should be lags where fixed kernel a's convolution is limiting. Initially use self c of a. The top self may not be top cross. But g solution made cross peaks active at lags top cross. Using cross for next call with same a may yield better g (if no update) but if we keep a unchanged, we need solve same LP with more constraints from cross. ```
def _optimize_self(a,S,max_time,rng,beta_init=10.0,beta_max=800.0):
    if max_time <=0: return a.copy(), _max_conv(a)*(2*len(a))/(S*S)
    n=len(a)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max_time
    y=_project_simplex(np.maximum(a,0.0),S)
    best_a=y.copy(); best_m=_max_conv(y)
    x_prev=y.copy()
    t=1.0; beta=float(beta_init); it=0; last=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       it+=1
       c=_self_conv(y); M=c.max()
       if M < best_m:
          best_m=M; best_a=y.copy(); last=it
       if it - last > 2500:
          scale=0.01*S/np.sqrt(n)*np.exp(-it/20000.0)
          if scale > 1e-8:
             y=_project_simplex(y + rng.normal(0.0,scale,n),S)
             x_prev=y.copy(); t=1.0; last=it
             beta=min(beta, 2*beta_init)
          else:
             break
       w,_= _lse_weights(c,beta)
       r=_corr_with_weights(w,y)
       rmax=np.max(r)
       if it % 300 == 0:
          lr=0.03
          if rmax>1e-12:
             y = y * np.exp(-lr*r/rmax)
             y = _project_simplex(y,S)
             x_prev=y.copy(); t=1.0
       step = 0.5/(beta*max(1.0, np.dot(y,y)))
       grad=2.0*r
       x=_project_simplex(y - step*grad, S)
       t_new=0.5*(1.0+np.sqrt(1.0+4.0*t*t))
       y = x + ((t-1.0)/t_new)*(x-x_prev)
       if y has negative? project? y=np.maximum(y,0); but projection may break sum if max. If any small negative due momentum, project.
       if np.any(y<0): y=_project_simplex(y,S)
       else: # ensure sum? FISTA combination of projected points with coefficients sum1, should sum S and nonneg? x and x_prev nonneg, coefficients sum? x + α(x-x_prev), α=(t-1)/t_new <1 positive. Sum S. Nonneg? Could be negative if α large and x-x_prev negative; max fix.
       x_prev=x; t=t_new
       beta=min(beta_max, beta+0.25)
    # final best maybe best_a
    return best_a, best_m * (2.0*n)/(S*S) ? Wait best_m stored max_conv not score. We can return max_conv and score separately.
```

```
def _prep_for_optimization(raw):
    arr=np.asarray(raw,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n=arr.size
    if n==0: return None
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    finite arr; if not finite replace 0.
    mx=np.max(arr)
    if mx<=0: return None
    shape=arr/mx
    sum_shape=float(np.sum(shape))
    if sum_shape <=0: return None
    target_scale=math.sqrt(2.0*n)/sum_shape
    if target_scale > 800.0 or not math.isfinite(target_scale): target_scale=800.0
    # If target_scale very small? ok. If sum = target_scale*sum_shape maybe <0.01? if target_scale=800 but sum_shape extremely small, maybe <0.01. Then evaluate would inf. Need ensure final sum>0.01. If sum_shape<0.01/800=1.25e-5, invalid. In that case use uniform.
    a=shape*target_scale
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 0.05:
        a=np.full(n, math.sqrt(2.0*n)/n)
        S=float(a.sum())
    if a.max()>1000.0:
        a*=999.0/a.max(); S=float(a.sum())
    return a,S
```
For optimal sequences sum S ~ sqrt(2n) or 800*sum_shape maybe large. If S >1e6? For shape sum maybe n, target_scale sqrt(2n)/n ~sqrt(2/n) small; S~sqrt(2n). If shape sparse sum small, cap 800, S maybe <800*sum_shape. If sum_shape can be up to n (if all ones), target_scale small; if shape has max1 but many zeros sum_shape small cap. S maybe >? If sum_shape=0.001, target_scale=800, S=0.8 >0.05. ok.

For generated central shape sum ~100, target_scale~1.25, S~128.

Now `_prepareừng interpolation`:
```
def _interp_prepare(a, new_n):
    n=len(a)
    if n==1: shape=np.ones(1);
    mx=np.max(a)
    if mx<=0: shape=a
    else: shape=a/mx
    t_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    t_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
    arr=np.interp(t_new,t_old,shape)
    return _prep_for_optimization(arr)
```
`np.interp` works with lists.

Now `_try_blend(a,g,S,t_list)` returns best candidate/score or None. Use max_conv.
```
def _blend_candidates(a,g,S,ts):
    n=len(a); const=2.0*n/(S*S)
    best_m=inf; best_cand=None
    for t in ts:
       cand=(1-t)*a + t*g
       cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0)
       cand=_project_simplex(cand,S)
       m=_max_conv(cand)
       if m<best_m: best_m=m; best_cand=cand.copy()
    if best_cand is None or not finite: return None
    return best_cand, best_m*const
```
In LP, we need compare to current score. Good.

`_top_indices(values,k)`:
```
def _top_indices(values,k):
    n=values.size
    k=int(min(k,n))
    if k<=0: return np.array([],dtype=int)
    if k==n: return np.arange(n)
    idx=np.argpartition(values, -k)[-k:]
    idx=idx[np.argsort(values[idx])[::-1]]
    return idx.astype(int)
```
No existing.

`_lp_best_response` needs `A_ub` rows. Since b length n, A_idx length m. If m too large maybe 200 cap. We'll cap before call.
```
def _lp_best_response(b,S,A_idx, time_limit=15.0):
    if not LP_AVAIL or len(A_idx)==0: return None
    n=b.size; m=len(A_idx)
    if m > 256: A_idx=A_idx[:256]; m=256
    rows=[]
    for k in A_idx:
        k=int(k)
        lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(k,n-1)
        if hi<lo: continue
        seg=b[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
        if np.max(seg) <=1e-14: continue
        rows.append((lo,hi,seg))
    if not rows: return None
    A=np.zeros((len(rows),n+1),dtype=float)
    for r,(lo,hi,seg) in enumerate(rows):
        A[r,lo:hi+1]=seg
        A[r,n]= -1.0
    Aeq=np.zeros((1,n+1),dtype=float); Aeq[0,:n]=1.0
    bvec=np.array([S],dtype=float)
    cvec=np.zeros(n+1,dtype=float); cvec[n]=1.0
    bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n + [(0.0,None)]
    last_exc=None
    for method in ('highs-ds','highs-ip','highs'):
        try:
            res=_linprog(cvec,A_ub=A,b_ub=np.zeros(len(rows)),A_eq=Aeq,b_eq=bvec,bounds=bounds,method=method,
                         options={'time_limit': max(0.1, float(time_limit)), 'presolve': 'on'})
            if res is not None and res.x is not None:
                if res.success:
                    x=np.asarray(res.x[:n],float)
                    if np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                        x=np.maximum(x,0.0)
                        if x.sum()>0.5*S:
                            x=_project_simplex(x,S)
                            return x
                # if timeout but x finite and feasible maybe use? check constraints lightly? Skip.
            if res is not None and getattr(res,'success',False):
                break
        except Exception as e:
            last_exc=e
            continue
    return None
```
Options 'presolve' maybe expects bool or string? HiGHS option presolve can be 'on'/'off' or bool. Use `True` to be safe? SciPy maps. Use `options={'time_limit':...'}`, omit presolve to avoid issues. But presolve default on. Use only time_limit. If time_limit unsupported, exception; then next method without options? Better two try: first with options, if exception then without. Code complex. Simpler: no options; rely on fast. But time limit safety important. Could pass `options={'time_limit': time_limit}` and if exception due key, catch and run without options. We'll implement:
```
for method in methods:
    for use_opts in (True, False):
       kwargs={}
       if use_opts: kwargs['options']={'time_limit': flt}
       try: res=linprog(..., method=method, **kwargs); break
       except Exception: continue
       process
```
But if first with options succeeds, process; if fails but no exception due timeout, return None.

6 a2w0

Now `_lse_weights` returns weights and cmax. We don't need lse value.
```
def _lse_weights(c,beta):
    cm=float(np.max(c))
    z=beta*(c-cm)
    z=np.maximum(z,-700.0)
    e=np.exp(z)
    tot=float(np.sum(e))
    if tot<=0: return np.ones(len(c))/len(c), cm
    return e/tot, cm
```

    if max_time <= 0: return x0.copy(), _score_cross? Return cross_m

    x=_project_simplex(np.maximum(x0,0),S); v=x.copy(); t=1; beta=10; best_m=_fft_conv(b,x).max(); best_x=x.copy()
    Bnorm=max(1.0,float(np.dot(b,b)))
    last=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       it+=1
       c=_fft_conv(b,x); M=c.max()
       if M<best_m: best_m=M; best_x=x.copy(); last=it
       if it-last>2000:
          if rng is None or it>5000: break
          scale=0.005*S/np.sqrt(n)
          x=_project_simplex(x+rng.normal(0,scale,n),S); v=x.copy(); t=1; last=it
       w,_=_lse_weights(c,beta)
       r=_corr_with_weights(w,b)
       step=0.5/(beta*Bnorm)
       xn=_project_simplex(x-step*r,S)
       t_new=...
       x=xn+((t-1)/t_new)*(xn-v)
       x=np.maximum(x,0)
       if np.any(x<0): x=_project_simplex(x,S)
       v=xn; t=t_new
       beta=min(500,beta+0.2)
    return best_x,best_m
```

Function `_prep_for_optimization` returns tuple or None. For shape generated, pass np array. If raw None? return None.

Detailed code:

```
def _prep_for_optimization(raw):
    arr=np.asarray(raw,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n=arr.size
    if n<2:
       return None
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    mx=float(np.max(arr))
    if mx <= 1e-300:
       return None
    shape=arr/mx
    sum_shape=float(np.sum(shape))
    if sum_shape <= 1e-12:
       return None
    target_scale=math.sqrt(2.0*float(n))/sum_shape
    if not math.isfinite(target_scale) or target_scale > 800.0:
        target_scale=800.0
    a=shape*target_scale
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 0.05:
        a=np.full(n, math.sqrt(2.0*float(n))/float(n))
        S=float(np.sum(a))
    mx2=float(np.max(a))
    if mx2 > 999.0:
        a = a * (999.0/mx2)
        S=float(np.sum(a))
    return a,S
```
If target_scale capped 800 but S maybe >1000? max 800. If S<0.05 fallback uniform. Good.

methods = ['highs-ds','highs-ip','highs']
for method in methods:
    tried=False
    for use_opts in (True, False):
        if tried: break
        opts = {'time_limit': flt} if use_opts else {}
        try:
            res=_linprog(..., method=method, **({'options':opts} if use_opts else {}))
            tried=True
            break
        except Exception:
            continue
    if not tried: continue
    if res is not None and getattr(res,'x',None) is not None:
        x=np.asarray(res.x[:n],float)
        if np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            x=np.maximum(x,0)
            xs=x.sum()
            if xs > 0.5*S:
                x=_project_simplex(x,S)
                # optionally check active constraints? Could compute cross max; if not too high maybe return.
                return x

Now `_lp_improve` using time. Need avoid infinite if LP returns same.
```
def _lp_improve(a,S,max_time):
    if max_time<=0: return a.copy(), _score(a,S)
    n=len(a); deadline=time.monotonic()+max_time
    best_a=a.copy(); best_m=_max_conv(a); best_score=_score_from_m(best_m,n,S)
    active=_self_conv(a)
    K=80
    no=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       if K<32: K=32
       if K>2*n-2: K=2*n-2
       A_idx=_top_indices(active,K)
       t0=time.monotonic()
       g=_lp_best_response(a,S,A_idx, max(1.0, (deadline-time.monotonic())*0.5))
       dt=time.monotonic()-t0
       if g is None:
          if K>64: K=max(40,K//2)
          else: break
          continue
       # blend
       best_cand_m=best_m; best_cand=None
       ts=(0.05,0.1,0.15,0.2,0.3,0.4,0.5,0.7,0.9,1.0)
       for t in ts:
          cand=(1.0-t)*a + t*g
          cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0)
          cand=_project_simplex(cand,S)
          m=_max_conv(cand)
          if m < best_cand_m:
              best_cand_m=m; best_cand=cand
       if best_cand is not None and best_cand_m < best_m - 1e-12:
           a=best_cand.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); best_m=best_cand_m; best_score=_score_from_m(best_m,n,S)
           active=_self_conv(a)
           no=0
           if dt < 3.0 and K<220: K=min(2*n-1,K+16)
       else:
           no+=1
           if no>=6: break
           active=_fft_conv(a,g)
           if K<220: K=min(2*n-1,K+16)
       # if dt big reduce
       if dt > 10.0 and K>64: K=max(40,K//2)
    return best_a,best_score
```
If K grows to ~220, LP matrix 220*8193 ~7MB, ok. For 16384 ~33MB. Time maybe.

```
def _optimize_self(a,S,max_time,rng,beta_init=8.0,beta_max=600.0):
    if max_time<=0: return a.copy(), _score(a,S)
    n=len(a); deadline=...
    y=_project_simplex(np.maximum(a,0.0),S)
    best_a=y.copy(); best_m=_max_conv(y); best_score=...
    v=y.copy(); t=1.0; beta=beta_init; it=0; last=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       it+=1
       c=_self_conv(y); M=c.max()
       if M<best_m:
          best_m=M; best_a=y.copy(); last=it
       if it-last>3000:
          scale=0.008*S/math.sqrt(n)*math.exp(-it/30000.0)
          if scale>1e-9:
             y=_project_simplex(y+rng.normal(0.0,scale,n),S)
             v=y.copy(); t=1.0; last=it
             beta=min(beta,2.0*beta_init)
          else:
             break
       w,_=_lse_weights(c,beta)
       r=_corr_with_weights(w,y)
       rmax=float(np.max(r))
       if it % 250 == 0:
          if rmax>1e-12:
             lr=0.03
             y=y*np.exp(-lr*r/rmax)
             y=_project_simplex(y,S)
             v=y.copy(); t=1.0
       l2=float(np.dot(y,y))
       step=0.5/(beta*max(1.0,l2))
       x=_project_simplex(y - 2.0*step*r, S)
       t_new=0.5*(1.0+math.sqrt(1.0+4.0*t*t))
       y=x + ((t-1.0)/t_new)*(x-v)
       y=np.maximum(y,0.0)
       if np.any(y<0): y=_project_simplex(y,S)
       # check x every 20
       if it % 20 == 0:
           Mx=_max_conv(x)
           if Mx < best_m:
               best_m=Mx; best_a=x.copy(); last=it
       v=x; t=t_new
       beta=min(beta_max,beta+0.25)
    # final maybe best_a
    return best_a,_score_from_m(best_m,n,S)
```

def _score_from_m(m,n,S):

If evaluate_sequence returns finite but internal best maybe different due clip? fine.

Now note `math.isfinite(ver)` where ver may be numpy scalar; ok.

Now let's consider if `height_sequence_1` includes values >1000; `_prep` clips before max normalize. If many clipped, shape altered but best. ok.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(a*a) / sum(a)^2
where a*a is linear self-convolution.

Strategy:
- Use the previous best sequence if present, plus analytic central-binomial
  starts as fallbacks.
- Use active-set linear-programming best responses to the bilinear term
  (fixed a, optimize g minimizing max(a*g)) to move toward better basins.
- Use LSE-smoothed FISTA-style projection for the non-convex self objective.
- Interpolate to a longer length (up to 16384) and polish if time remains.

All functions are top-level and avoid closures/lambdas.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.fft import rfft as _rfft, irfft as _irfft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import rfft as _rfft, irfft as _irfft

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _linprog
    _LP_AVAILABLE = True
except Exception:
    _linprog = None
    _LP_AVAILABLE = False

_MAX_RETURN_N = 16384


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_conv(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0 or b.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    out = a.size + b.size - 1
    n = _next_pow2(out)
    A = _rfft(a, n)
    B = _rfft(b, n)
    c = _irfft(A * B, n)
    return c[:out]


def _self_conv(a):
    c = _fft_conv(a, a)
    # Convolution of non-negative data should be non-negative; remove tiny NEG
    # numerical artifacts.
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def _max_conv(a):
    c = _self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(c))


def _corr_weights_kernel(weights, kernel):
    """
    grad_i = sum_k weights[k] * kernel[k-i]
    for i=0,...,n-1, kernel length n, weights length 2n-1.
    """
    n = int(kernel.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    out = n + L - 1
    N = _next_pow2(out)
    wr = weights[::-1]
    A = _rfft(kernel, N)
    B = _rfft(wr, N)
    d = _irfft(A * B, N)
    return d[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]


def _lse_weights(c, beta):
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    z = beta * (c - cm)
    z = np.maximum(z, -700.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    tot = float(np.sum(e))
    if tot <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(tot):
        return np.ones(len(c), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, len(c)), cm
    return e / tot, cm


def _project_simplex(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(v.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(max(0.0, S))], dtype=np.float64)
    if S <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(np.sum(v))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if np.min(v) >= -1e-12 and abs(s - S) <= 1e-10 * max(1.0, abs(S)):
        v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
        return v * (S / float(np.sum(v)))
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = (css - S) / rho
    cond = theta <= u
    if not np.any(cond):
        r = n
    else:
        r = int(np.where(cond)[0][-1]) + 1
    theta = (css[r - 1] - S) / r
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)


def _prep_for_optimization(raw):
    """
    Convert an arbitrary sequence into a safe internal representation:
    nonnegative, max <= 800, sum > 0.01, and scaled so optimization is stable.
    """
    arr = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(arr.size)
    if n < 2:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    mx = float(np.max(arr))
    if mx <= 1e-300:
        return None
    shape = arr / mx
    sum_shape = float(np.sum(shape))
    if sum_shape <= 1e-12:
        return None

    target_scale = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n)) / sum_shape
    if not math.isfinite(target_scale) or target_scale > 800.0:
        target_scale = 800.0

    a = shape * target_scale
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 0.05 or not math.isfinite(S):
        a = np.full(n, math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n)) / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        S = float(np.sum(a))

    mx2 = float(np.max(a))
    if mx2 > 999.0:
        a = a * (999.0 / mx2)
        S = float(np.sum(a))

    return a, S


def _uniform_sequence(n):
    return _prep_for_optimization(np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64))


def _central_shape(n):
    """
    Coefficients of (1-x)^(-1/2) truncated to length n:
        c_j = binom(2j,j)/4^j.
    This is a natural polynomial approximation to a flat self-convolution.
    """
    n = int(n)
    c = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c[0] = 1.0
    for j in range(1, n):
        c[j] = c[j - 1] * (2.0 * j - 1.0) / (2.0 * j)
    return c


def _score_from_max(m, n, S):
    if S <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(m):
        return math.inf
    return float(2.0 * float(n) * m / (S * S))


def _score(a, S):
    return _score_from_max(_max_conv(a), len(a), S)


def _top_indices(values, k):
    values = np.asarray(values, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(values.size)
    k = int(min(max(0, k), n))
    if k <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=int)
    if k == n:
        return np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    idx = np.argpartition(values, -k)[-k:]
    idx = idx[np.argsort(values[idx])[::-1]]
    return idx.astype(int)


def _blend_best_candidate(a, g, S, current_m, ts=(0.05, 0.1, 0.15, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9, 1.0)):
    best_m = current_m
    best_cand = None
    for t in ts:
        cand = (1.0 - t) * a + t * g
        cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
        cand = _project_simplex(cand, S)
        m = _max_conv(cand)
        if m < best_m:
            best_m = m
            best_cand = cand.copy()
    if best_cand is None:
        return None, best_m
    return best_cand, best_m


def _lp_best_response(b, S, A_idx, time_limit=15.0):
    """
    Solve (approximately) the LP:
        min M
        s.t. (b*x)_k <= M for k in A_idx
             sum(x) = S, x >= 0.
    """
    if not _LP_AVAILABLE or len(A_idx) == 0:
        return None

    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(b.size)
    A_idx = np.asarray(A_idx, dtype=int)

    # Keep LP size sane.
    if A_idx.size > 240:
        vals = _self_conv(b)  # not used if A_idx already selected, but fallback
        A_idx = _top_indices(vals, 240)
    if A_idx.size < 1:
        return None

    rows = []
    for k in A_idx:
        k = int(k)
        if k < 0 or k >= 2 * n - 1:
            continue
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(k, n - 1)
        if hi < lo:
            continue
        seg = b[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]
        if np.max(seg) <= 1e-14:
            continue
        rows.append((lo, hi, seg))

    if not rows:
        return None

    m = len(rows)
    A = np.zeros((m, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    for r, (lo, hi, seg) in enumerate(rows):
        A[r, lo:hi + 1] = seg
        A[r, n] = -1.0

    Aeq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    Aeq[0, :n] = 1.0
    bvec = np.array([float(S)], dtype=np.float64)

    cvec = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cvec[n] = 1.0

    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n + [(0.0, None)]
    b_ub = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)

    methods = ("highs-ds", "highs-ip", "highs")
    flt_time = float(max(0.5, time_limit))

    for method in methods:
        solved = False
        for use_opts in (True, False):
            if solved:
                break
            kwargs = {}
            if use_opts:
                kwargs["options"] = {"time_limit": flt_time}
            try:
                res = _linprog(
                    cvec,
                    A_ub=A,
                    b_ub=b_ub,
                    A_eq=Aeq,
                    b_eq=bvec,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    method=method,
                    **kwargs
                )
                solved = True
            except Exception:
                continue

        if not solved:
            continue

        if res is None:
            continue
        x = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if x is None:
            continue
        x = np.asarray(x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            continue
        x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
        xs = float(np.sum(x))
        if xs <= 0.5 * max(1e-12, S):
            continue
        x = _project_simplex(x, S)
        return x

    return None


def _lp_improve(a, S, max_time):
    """
    Active-set best-response improvement for the self objective.
    Fixed a, solve LP for g minimizing max(a*g) on active lags, then blend.
    """
    if max_time <= 0.0:
        return a.copy(), _score(a, S)

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max_time
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = _max_conv(a)
    best_score = _score_from_max(best_m, n, S)

    active = _self_conv(a)
    K = 80
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if K < 40:
            K = 40
        if K > 2 * n - 1:
            K = 2 * n - 1

        A_idx = _top_indices(active, K)
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        g = _lp_best_response(a, S, A_idx, max(1.0, (deadline - t0) * 0.5))
        dt = time.monotonic() - t0

        if g is None:
            if K > 64:
                K = max(40, K // 2)
            else:
                break
            continue

        cand, m = _blend_best_candidate(a, g, S, best_m)
        if cand is not None and m < best_m - 1e-12:
            a = cand
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_m = m
            best_score = _score_from_max(best_m, n, S)
            active = _self_conv(a)
            no_improve = 0
            if dt < 3.0 and K < 240:
                K = min(2 * n - 1, K + 16)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 6:
                break
            active = _fft_conv(a, g)
            if K < 240:
                K = min(2 * n - 1, K + 16)

        if dt > 10.0 and K > 64:
            K = max(40, K // 2)

    return best_a, best_score


def _best_response_fista(b, x0, S, max_time, rng=None):
    """
    Convex FISTA/LSE solver for fixed b:
        min_x max_k (b*x)_k, sum(x)=S, x>=0.
    Returns x and the achieved max(b*x).
    """
    if max_time <= 0.0:
        x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64)
        x = _project_simplex(np.maximum(x0, 0.0), S)
        return x, float(np.max(_fft_conv(b, x)))

    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(b)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max_time

    x = _project_simplex(np.maximum(x0, 0.0), S)
    v = x.copy()
    t_mom = 1.0
    beta = 10.0
    Bnorm = max(1.0, float(np.dot(b, b)))

    best_x = x.copy()
    best_m = float(np.max(_fft_conv(b, x)))
    last_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        c = _fft_conv(b, x)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M < best_m:
            best_m = M
            best_x = x.copy()
            last_improve = it

        if it - last_improve > 2500:
            if rng is None or it > 6000:
                break
            scale = 0.005 * S / math.sqrt(float(n))
            x = _project_simplex(x + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n), S)
            v = x.copy()
            t_mom = 1.0
            last_improve = it
            continue

        w, _ = _lse_weights(c, beta)
        r = _corr_weights_kernel(w, b)
        step = 0.5 / (beta * Bnorm)

        xn = _project_simplex(x - step * r, S)
        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + math.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t_mom * t_mom))
        x = xn + ((t_mom - 1.0) / t_new) * (xn - v)
        x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
        if abs(float(np.sum(x)) - S) > 1e-9:
            x = _project_simplex(x, S)

        v = xn
        t_mom = t_new
        beta = min(800.0, beta + 0.25)

    M = float(np.max(_fft_conv(b, best_x)))
    return best_x, M


def _response_improve(a, S, max_time, rng=None):
    """
    Improved best-response iteration without LP:
    g = argmin_g max(a*g), then blend g into a.
    """
    if max_time <= 0.0:
        return a.copy(), _score(a, S)

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max_time
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = _max_conv(a)
    best_score = _score_from_max(best_m, n, S)
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        chunk = min(20.0, 0.35 * rem)
        if chunk < 2.0:
            break

        g, _ = _best_response_fista(a, a, S, chunk, rng)
        if g is None:
            break

        cand, m = _blend_best_candidate(a, g, S, best_m)
        if cand is not None and m < best_m - 1e-12:
            a = cand
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_m = m
            best_score = _score_from_max(best_m, n, S)
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 6:
                break

    return best_a, best_score


def _optimize_self(a, S, max_time, rng):
    """
    LSE-smoothed FISTA-style local search for the original self objective.
    """
    if max_time <= 0.0:
        return a.copy(), _score(a, S)

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max_time

    y = _project_simplex(np.maximum(a, 0.0), S)
    best_a = y.copy()
    best_m = _max_conv(y)
    best_score = _score_from_max(best_m, n, S)

    v = y.copy()
    t_mom = 1.0
    beta = 8.0
    it = 0
    last_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        c = _self_conv(y)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M < best_m:
            best_m = M
            best_a = y.copy()
            best_score = _score_from_max(best_m, n, S)
            last_improve = it

        if it - last_improve > 3000:
            scale = 0.008 * S / math.sqrt(float(n)) * math.exp(-it / 30000.0)
            if scale > 1e-9:
                y = _project_simplex(y + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n), S)
                v = y.copy()
                t_mom = 1.0
                last_improve = it
                beta = min(beta, 2.0 * 8.0)
                continue
            break

        w, _ = _lse_weights(c, beta)
        r = _corr_weights_kernel(w, y)
        rmax = float(np.max(r))

        if it % 250 == 0:
            if rmax > 1e-12:
                lr = 0.03
                y = y * np.exp(-lr * r / rmax)
                y = _project_simplex(y, S)
                v = y.copy()
                t_mom = 1.0
                continue

        l2 = float(np.dot(y, y))
        step = 0.5 / (beta * max(1.0, l2))
        x = _project_simplex(y - 2.0 * step * r, S)

        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + math.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t_mom * t_mom))
        y = x + ((t_mom - 1.0) / t_new) * (x - v)
        y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
        if abs(float(np.sum(y)) - S) > 1e-9:
            y = _project_simplex(y, S)

        if it % 20 == 0:
            Mx = _max_conv(x)
            if Mx < best_m:
                best_m = Mx
                best_a = x.copy()
                best_score = _score_from_max(best_m, n, S)
                last_improve = it

        v = x
        t_mom = t_new
        beta = min(800.0, beta + 0.25)

    return best_a, best_score


def _interp_prepare(a, new_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    mx = float(np.max(a))
    if mx <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(mx):
        shape = np.zeros_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.size > 0:
            shape[0] = 1.0
    else:
        shape = a / mx

    if a.size == 1:
        arr = np.full(int(new_n), shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        t_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
        t_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(new_n))
        arr = np.interp(t_new, t_old, shape)

    return _prep_for_optimization(arr)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    if not math.isfinite(total_budget) or total_budget <= 60.0:
        total_budget = 2400.0
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(60.0, total_budget - 45.0)

    best_a = None
    best_S = None
    best_score = math.inf

    def _consider(a, S):
        nonlocal best_a, best_S, best_score
        if a is None or S is None:
            return
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.size < 2:
            return
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if abs(float(np.sum(a)) - float(S)) > 1e-8 * max(1.0, abs(float(S))):
            S = float(np.sum(a))
        sc = _score(a, S)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_S = float(S)

    # 1) Try to load previous best sequence.
    given = None
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1
        prep = _prep_for_optimization(raw)
        if prep is not None:
            a, S = prep
            if len(a) > _MAX_RETURN_N:
                prep2 = _interp_prepare(a, _MAX_RETURN_N)
                if prep2 is not None:
                    a, S = prep2
            given = (a, S)
    except Exception:
        given = None

    candidates = []
    if given is not None:
        candidates.append(given)

    # 2) Analytic fallback candidates.
    for n_candidate in (2048, 8192):
        try:
            cand = _prep_for_optimization(_central_shape(n_candidate))
            if cand is not None:
                candidates.append(cand)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Pick the best initial candidate.
    for cand in candidates:
        if cand is None:
            continue
        a, S = cand
        _consider(a, S)

    if best_a is None:
        prep = _uniform_sequence(2048)
        best_a, best_S = prep

    # 3) Best-response / LP phase at current length.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 30.0:
        a_cur, S_cur = best_a, best_S
        lp_time = min(300.0, 0.30 * remaining)
        if _LP_AVAILABLE and len(a_cur) <= _MAX_RETURN_N and lp_time > 20.0:
            try:
                a1, sc1 = _lp_improve(a_cur, S_cur, lp_time)
                _consider(a1, S_cur if a1 is not best_a else S_cur)
                if a1 is not None and sc1 < best_score:
                    best_a, best_S = a1, S_cur
            except Exception:
                pass

        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        resp_time = min(220.0, 0.22 * remaining)
        if resp_time > 20.0:
            try:
                a1, sc1 = _response_improve(best_a, best_S, resp_time, rng)
                _consider(a1, best_S)
                if a1 is not None and sc1 < best_score:
                    best_a, best_S = a1, best_S
            except Exception:
                pass

    # 4) Direct self-convolution local search at current length.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    self_time = min(700.0, 0.40 * remaining)
    if self_time > 20.0:
        try:
            a1, sc1 = _optimize_self(best_a, best_S, self_time, rng)
            _consider(a1, best_S)
            if a1 is not None and sc1 < best_score:
                best_a, best_S = a1, best_S
        except Exception:
            pass

    # 5) Longer-length refinement (up to 16384).
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 140.0 and best_a is not None:
        new_n = 16384
        if best_a.size >= new_n:
            new_n = int(best_a.size)
        starts = []

        try:
            prep_interp = _interp_prepare(best_a, new_n)
            if prep_interp is not None:
                starts.append(prep_interp)
        except Exception:
            pass

        if remaining > 300.0:
            try:
                prep_central = _prep_for_optimization(_central_shape(new_n))
                if prep_central is not None:
                    starts.append(prep_central)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if starts:
            per_start = max(20.0, 0.80 * remaining / max(1, len(starts)))
            for st in starts:
                if st is None:
                    continue
                a0, S0 = st
                # Early quick check; if the start is already worse by a lot,
                # still allow limited optimization only if enough time remains.
                sc0 = _score(a0, S0)
                if not math.isfinite(sc0):
                    continue
                budget_for_start = min(per_start, max(10.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.9))
                if budget_for_start < 25.0:
                    continue
                try:
                    a1, sc1 = _optimize_self(a0, S0, budget_for_start, rng)
                    _consider(a1, S0)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                # Optional short LP on the new best if length=16384 and time remains.
                remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if (
                    remaining > 60.0
                    and _LP_AVAILABLE
                    and best_a is not None
                    and best_a.size <= _MAX_RETURN_N
                ):
                    lp_time = min(120.0, 0.35 * remaining)
                    if lp_time > 20.0:
                        try:
                            a2, sc2 = _lp_improve(best_a, best_S, lp_time)
                            _consider(a2, best_S)
                        except Exception:
                            pass

    # Final safety: ensure returnable sequence.
    if best_a is None:
        best_a, best_S = _uniform_sequence(2048)

    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64)
    if best_a.size > _MAX_RETURN_N:
        try:
            prep = _interp_prepare(best_a, _MAX_RETURN_N)
            if prep is not None:
                best_a, best_S = prep
        except Exception:
            best_a = np.clip(best_a[:_MAX_RETURN_N], 0.0, 1000.0)

    if best_a.size < 2:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)

    # Keep values safely within evaluator clipping range.
    final_arr = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s_final = float(np.sum(final_arr))
    if s_final < 0.05:
        final_arr = np.full(1024, math.sqrt(2.0 * 1024) / 1024.0, dtype=np.float64)

    final_list = [float(x) for x in final_arr]

    # Verify if evaluator is available. If it rejects, fallback to uniform.
    try:
        ver = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
    except Exception:
        ver = np.inf

    if not math.isfinite(ver):
        final_arr = np.full(1024, math.sqrt(2.0 * 1024) / 1024.0, dtype=np.float64)
        final_list = [float(x) for x in final_arr]
        try:
            evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        except Exception:
            pass

    print("length=%d, internal_score=%.8f, best_score=%.8f" % (
        len(final_list), _score(final_arr, float(np.sum(final_arr))), best_score
    ))
    return final_list
```
